# Master Page Creation & Planning Registry (plan.md)

This file is the single source of truth for planning, creating, and verifying all pages from `urls.txt`.

---

## Project Workflow & Governance Protocol

1. **Strictly Manual Execution:** No page generation scripts or automated generation allowed. All PHP files must be created and verified manually.
2. **Batch Enforcement:** Work in strict batches of **5–10 pages maximum**.
3. **Planning Guarantee:** Every URL must have a complete planning record in `plan.md` marked as `Not Started` or `In Progress` before file creation.
4. **Title Format Protocol:**
   - **Service / Location / Route / Hub Pages:** `Main Keyword | 8409531615`
   - **Blog / Guide / Informational Pages:** Natural informational title (NO phone number).
5. **Meta Keywords Mandate:** Exactly **15 unique, relevant keywords** planned per page.
6. **Mandatory Content Word Count Rule:** Every created page MUST achieve a strict minimum content word density of **1,800+ true words** (target 2,000+ words) following the standardized 11-section architectural template.
7. **Strict Governance & Rule Check Mandate:** Before and during page creation, every rule in `plan.md` MUST be thoroughly reviewed and strictly followed. Do NOT ignore rules, make unverified assumptions, or introduce new unapproved modifications mid-work.
8. **Technical & On-Page SEO Mandate:** Every page must be fully optimized for Technical and On-Page SEO:
   - Proper Canonical Tag matching exact URL.
   - Unique, descriptive Title Tag and Meta Description.
   - Complete JSON-LD Schema Markup (LocalBusiness, MovingCompany, FAQPage, BreadcrumbList).
   - Strict Heading Hierarchy (single H1 per page, sequential H2/H3 structure).
   - Semantic HTML5 structure with responsive UI layout.
   - Unique interactive IDs and valid internal link structures.
9. **Verification Protocol:** Mark status as `Completed` and subsequently `Verified` only after manual code inspection, word count audit, and syntax check.
